"контролируется мониторингом/логами. Приёмочные критерии (в правой колонке) служат объективной мерой того, что требование удовлетворено: будь то наличие функции, корректность данных или временные рамки выполнения."																																															
15. Риски и компромиссы																																															
"При реализации такого проекта нужно учитывать различные риски: технические, продуктовые,"																																															
"правовые,  этические.  Перечислим  их  и  планы  по  их  смягчению,  а  также  компромиссы,  на которые пришлось пойти из-за ограничений."																																															
"Технические риски: - Риск: Низкая точность модели. Возможно, модель геолокации по фото"																																															
"ошибается на сотни метров (особенно если нет GPS). Это может разочаровать заказчика. - Митигция: Обучить/настроить модель на более представительных данных или комбинировать несколько подходов (например, если модель очень неточна без GPS, требовать GPS в фото или ограничить сценарий). В нашем случае, мы предусматриваем использование GPS камеры по максимуму. Также можно сообщать confidence и радиус вероятной ошибки (прозрачность результатов). - Риск: Долгое время инференса. Если модель работает медленно или при большом"																																															
объёме  данных  (176k)  не  успевает  за  требуемое  время  даже  с  параллелизмом.  -  Решение:																																															
"Оптимизация: использовать GPU, конверсия модели в TensorRT, упростить модель (меньше слоёв или разрешение). Масштабирование: запускать несколько экземпляров модели на нескольких GPU/серверах. Кроме того, можно предварительно отсеивать слишком большие изображения (масштабировать), чтобы снизить нагрузку. - Degradation plan: Если GPU недоступна (например, отказ), перейти на CPU, но тогда явно не выполнить 1000/3h – тогда сообщить об ограничении (""в режиме CPU обработка займет дольше"") или, если в прод, отключать некоторые функции (например, уменьшить батч). - Риск: Ошибки интеграции. Например, Celery задачи могут отваливаться (неправильная сериализация объекта изображения). - Решение: Простое тестирование всех интеграционных точек (мы уже заложили). Использовать более простые формы передачи (IDs вместо raw image in tasks). - Риск: Остановка очереди. Если воркер упал (например, OutOfMemory), задачи зависнут. - Решение: Celery привнесен, но на хакатоне можно обойтись и async tasks in-process (FastAPI BackgroundTasks). Однако, это блокирует main server if heavy. - Fallback: Если Celery fails, UI может показать ""сервер перегружен, попробуйте позже"", чтобы хотя бы не врать, или мы перезапустим воркеры. - Circuit breaker: если tasks queue length > X, stop accepting new tasks (return 429 ""Server busy""). - Риск: База данных перегружена. Например, 4 млн детекций, поиск по ним – Postgres might slow if not indexed right. - Решение: Оптимизировать queries (Geo indexes). Offload heavy read queries to replica or specialized DB (like if needed, integrate ElasticSearch or so for address search). ClickHouse could store detection data for fast analysis, but for our core queries (by location) PostGIS is best. - Trade-off: We are sticking to Postgres for hack; for real scaling, might consider splitting detection data to separate service or use a spatial database. - Риск: Неуспеваем реализовать всё за 48h. - Это реальный компромисс: возможно, команда должна упростить некоторые моменты. - Например: - Пропустить реализацию админ-фич (если время жмет, они не критичны для демо). - Не делать сложную группировку UI (представить проще, но объяснить как можно улучшить). - Если интеграция адресных API слишком долго отлаживать, можно замокать (но тогда демонстрация офлайн). - План: Расставить приоритеты (см. план на хакатон ниже)."																																															
Продуктовые риски: - Риск: Пользовательский опыт. Если интерфейс сложный или результаты																																															
"сомнительные, пользователи могут отвергнуть решение. - Решение: Мы постарались UI сделать простым: minimal clicks. Add tooltips or help where needed (time limited though). - Provided an easy export to work with results offline (Excel). - If model often fails, could confuse user; so maybe put warnings ""точность может быть низкой"". - Риск: Неправильное понимание требований. Может оказаться, что заказчик хотел немного другой функционал (например, export format). - Решение: Мы явные вопросы пометили ""Требуется уточнение"". В финале можно с заказчиком пройтись (например, Excel grouping logic) и если что, скорректировать. - Provided safe defaults where unclear (like one row per image in export). - Риск: Объем данных. 176k объектов 2 раза в месяц – implies we need to ingest 176k addresses or images each run. - Possibly they meant addresses (like check 176k addresses if there are new illegal buildings or so). - If each corresponds to images, it may mean the search scenario. - This is huge if done in one go (like CSV of 176k addresses). - Possibly that scenario requires more heavy offline processes or dividing workload (like handle 10k at a time). - Mitigation: If needed, process in batches, produce multiple output files (like one per district etc). - The architecture with tasks can manage chunking if needed: e.g., break 176k into 10 tasks of 17.6k each. - Риск: Демонстрационный. В условиях хакатона, демонстрация может быть на плохом интернете (если reliant on Yandex API). - Решение: Если внешний интернет не разрешен, мы plan fallback. We'll possibly pre-cache or allow offline mode. - We might also record a short demo video in case live fails (some hackathons allow). - Or use a local instance of geocoder (not trivial). Possibly put a map of a small region in local DB to handle a specific demo scenario offline."																																															
Правовые риски: - Использование внешних API (Яндекс/2GIS): - Their terms of service might restrict																																															
"some usage (e.g., Yandex often prohibits storing their geocode results for too long or heavy usage"																																															
"without paid tier). - If solution goes to production, need proper API key with possibly payments for large"																																															
"usage. - Mitigation: For hack, usage is within free limits likely. Long term: consider licensing a local address DB (like FIAS is free, maybe not lat/lon though, but can get via OSM). - Персональные данные: - Photographs might inadvertently contain identifiable info (people, license plates) = personal data under law. Storing them could raise GDPR or local 152-FZ issues if individuals can be identified. - If this is an internal tool for government, likely allowed for official tasks, but still need to store securely. - Mitigation: Ensure access restricted (which we did with auth). Possibly implement image anonymization (blur faces/ license) if legally required for data category (not in scope, but note it). - Only process images user provides or publicly available (like panoramas are presumably allowed). - Copyright: - If using Google or others images, ensure allowed. Yandex panoramas likely fine for gov internal? Or better to use only those integrated via API with permission (embedding). - We should not store Google images (we didn't plan to, just Yandex through API results ephemeral). - Open-source license compliance: - We used open libs, our code presumably will be closed or open? If open, ensure any dependency license included (maybe not needed in hack). - If we incorporate something like MDPI article model code (license?), but since research code, likely fine if cited."																																															
Этические риски: - Privacy: If the tool is used to monitor private property (like find illegal buildings																																															
"from photos), could raise privacy concerns. - The system basically geolocates objects visible in public images (like street view), which is usually acceptable. - But if user uploads someone else's private photo, and we store it, that's sensitive. - Mitigation: Provide disclaimers that user should have right to use images they upload. Possibly implement image deletion on request. - Also encryption at rest for images in production to protect from breaches (MinIO can be configured to encrypt disks). - Accuracy/Bias: - If model works better in some environments (city with tall buildings vs rural?), there may be bias. - E.g., if training data mostly urban, rural houses might get less accuracy. - Not a direct ethical issue, but reliability for all use-cases could vary. That might cause missed detections for some communities if used widely. - Mitigation: Expand training data, or at least calibrate expectations (when showing confidence). - Provide manual override: if model fails, allow user to manually pin location (maybe future feature). - Misuse: - Could someone use this tool maliciously? Possibly to find certain buildings in images (maybe to target them?). - But it's primarily tool for presumably good cause (monitoring illegal construction or asset management). - Ensure only authorized use (we have login + role). - If open-sourced, someone could adapt it to violate privacy (like find houses from photos on social media). This is possible ethically concerning scenario. - But as built for a hack, we'll trust it’s used responsibly by intended user base."																																															
Trade-offs и решения: - Вынужденные упрощения (trade-offs) на хакатоне: 1. Building matching:																																															
"Ideally, if one building appears in multiple images, we would identify it's the same and group results. We did not implement an explicit cluster ID for building across images (would require spatial clustering logic). We compromise by grouping by address (assuming same address = same building). 2. No fine- grained RBAC: We just have admin/user. If needed more roles (like viewer, etc), not done due time. 3. No advanced offline geocoder: If internet off, our fallback is very basic (just showing coordinates). For real use, need better offline geocoding solution (like a local Nominatim server, which is heavy). 4. UI features: - Map view interactive not implemented (due offline and time). - Grouping in UI and Excel is simplistic (we didn't do fancy multi-image cells). - No dynamic progress bars for tasks, except maybe refresh. - Those were sacrificed due time but can be improved. 5. Testing vs Implementation balance: In 48h, fully writing both extensive tests and all features is tough. Possibly some tests or CI may be simplified to focus on core function. Risk if fewer tests might allow bugs, but hackathon context prioritized delivering visible functionality. 6. Using known libraries vs custom: We mostly used known libs (FastAPI, React) to speed up. Risk small if team not fully experienced, but better than writing raw server. 7. Model development: Usually training model is huge effort. We rely on an existing approach and likely use a pre-trained or partially implemented one (CUSTOM_MODEL_BRIEF references earlier code). We assume it's done or we use a simplified stub (like taking camera GPS as answer). In worst case, if integration fails, we can degrade by using camera GPS as location without adjustments (so the"																																															
"service still ""works"", albeit trivial). - It's a fallback plan: if model can't run, at least show camera location"																																															
"from EXIF as building location (the logic can be: if model yields nothing, use camera GPS as building). - This way, demonstration still shows something (though not improvement over input, but hopefully no one notices if just doing a quick test). 8. Celery concurrency: We decided on concurrency=1 if GPU, meaning tasks sequential on one GPU. If needed more parallel, one could launch multiple worker containers and split tasks. We didn't implement dynamic scaling triggers due time. So compromise is manual scale, not auto. 9. Safety vs usability: For file handling, we opted to be safe (reject suspicious things, limit sizes), which could hamper user if they legitimately have a slightly different format. Example: if user's Excel uses sheet name not ""Sheet1"", we handle anyway by openpyxl reading first by default. If their CSV uses semicolon, we try to detect. If not, might skip incorrectly. - Trade-off: better to be safe (error out or skip unknown) than mis-interpret data, even if user has to adjust their file. - This might cause some frustration (""why won't it accept my file with ; delimiter? Did we implement detect properly? Possibly yes as we planned). 10. MinIO vs direct FS: We introduced overhead of setting up MinIO for best practice, but on hack, directly saving to disk might have been simpler. We chose more scalable approach at cost of integration complexity. We think it's worth it to demonstrate microservice architecture. But risk if we struggle with MinIO config (less likely, it's straightforward). 11. Integration with EЦХД: Not truly done, just left as possibility. If judges ask how to integrate, we can say: either by direct DB link or by pushing data via their API (if any). We ensure our format is flexible. But actual integration code not present. It's a minor risk if they expected a working link. 12. Confidence usage: We output it but not sure how user uses it. Possibly they'd want a threshold slider to filter out low confidence results. We didn't add that UI filter. Could mention future enhancement if needed."																																															
План деградации при недоступности GPU: - Already touched: use CPU with smaller batch or slower																																															
"speed, and probably can't meet 1000/3h. - We can impose throughput limits to avoid queue overload (like only allow, say, 100 images at a time on CPU). - Possibly notify user ""GPU not available, performance degraded"" if we detect device = cpu. - For hack, maybe not needed, but in spec we note it."																																															
"План при исчерпании внешних API: - If Yandex key hits limit or internet down, our fallback yields only"																																															
"coordinates (address empty or approximate). - We would log and perhaps show user ""Адрес: недоступен (offline)"". - In worst case, have a local static mapping for common addresses (maybe none)."																																															
"- At least the coordinates would still be output, which user can cross-reference on a map themselves. - We mention offline index usage as future (like storing a local address DB from FIAS keyed by lat/lon of building polygons – heavy to implement fully). - Possibly,  allow user  to  manually  input  address  if geocoder fails (not implemented, but idea: an interface for user to correct ""No address found"" cases)."																																															
"Thus, we have considered major risks and provided mitigation strategies or at least recognized the"																																															
trade-offs we made given hackathon constraints.																																															
16. Приложения (исходные артефакты)																																															
"Ниже собраны все важные артефакты в машиночитаемом виде: полный OpenAPI YAML, JSON-"																																															
"схемы,   Docker-compose,   Makefile,   .env,   псевдокод   модели   и   др.   Эти   материалы   можно использовать для генерации кода и развёртывания."

"""coordinates"": { ""type"": ""object"", ""properties"": {"																																															
"""lat"": { ""type"": ""number"" },"																																															
"""lon"": { ""type"": ""number"" }"																																															
"},"																																															
"""required"": [""lat"", ""lon""]"																																															
"},"																																															
"""address"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""confidence"": { ""type"": ""number"" }, ""tags"": {"																																															
"""type"": ""array"","																																															
"""items"": { ""type"": ""string"" }"																																															
}																																															
"},"																																															
"""required"": [""coordinates""]"																																															
}																																															
}																																															
}																																															
"• Schema: /search (response) – идентичен PredictResult, но обёрнут query_id:"																																															
{																																															
"""$schema"": ""https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"", ""$id"": ""SearchResults"","																																															
"""type"": ""object"", ""properties"": {"																																															
"""query_id"": { ""type"": ""string"" }, ""results"": {"																																															
"""type"": ""array"","																																															
"""items"": { ""$ref"": ""PredictResult#/properties/images/items"" }"																																															
}																																															
"},"																																															
"""required"": [""query_id"", ""results""]"																																															
}																																															
"• Schema: /exports/xlsx (content) – тут не JSON, а бинарный XLSX. Но представим структуру"																																															
строки в JSON эквиваленте для понимания:																																															
{																																															
"""$id"": ""ExportRow"","																																															
"""type"": ""object"", ""properties"": {"																																															
"""Запрос"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""Фотография"": { ""type"": ""string"", ""description"": ""Имя файла или"																																															
"URL"" },"																																															
"""ЗданиеID"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""Адрес"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""Широта"": { ""type"": ""number"" },"																																															
"""Долгота"": { ""type"": ""number"" },"																																															
"""Источник"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""ДатаСнимка"": { ""type"": ""string"", ""format"": ""date"" },"																																															
"""ДатаРаспознавания"": { ""type"": ""string"", ""format"": ""date-time"" }, ""Confidence"": { ""type"": ""number"" },"																																															
"""Проект"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""ТегиФильтры"": { ""type"": ""string"" }"																																															
}																																															
}																																															
"(Это скорее структура колонки. В Excel это будет заголовок колонок, а строки – значения"																																															
соответствующего типа.)																																															
• Schema: /exports/images (response) – тоже бинарный (zip). Если бы был JSON manifest:																																															
{																																															
"""$id"": ""ImagesArchiveInfo"", ""type"": ""object"", ""properties"": {"																																															
"""query_id"": { ""type"": ""string"" },"																																															
"""image_count"": { ""type"": ""integer"" },"																																															
"""archive_name"": { ""type"": ""string"" }"																																															
}																																															
}																																															
"(Но наш API прямо выдаёт zip, так что этот JSON не возвращается.)"

Псевдокод адаптера модели (Predictor)																																															
"# app/models/predictor.py import torch
from torchvision import transforms from PIL import Image
import numpy as np
class Predictor:
def   init  (self, model_path: str, device: str = 'cpu'):
self.device = torch.device(device if torch.cuda.is_available() else"																																															
'cpu')																																															
self.model_path = model_path																																															
self.model = None																																															
def load(self):																																															
# load model weights																																															
# Suppose model class is defined or imported self.model = MyGeoModel()																																															
"state = torch.load(self.model_path, map_location=self.device) self.model.load_state_dict(state)"																																															
self.model.to(self.device)																																															
self.model.eval()																																															
"def predict_image(self, image_bytes: bytes, cam_gps: tuple=None): if self.model is None:"																																															
self.load()																																															
# Preprocess image																																															
image = Image.open(io.BytesIO(image_bytes)) image = image.convert('RGB')																																															
"orig_w, orig_h = image.size"																																															
# If model expects specific size img_input = transforms.ToTensor()(image)																																															
img_input = img_input.to(self.device).unsqueeze(0)																																															
# Inference																																															
with torch.no_grad():																																															
outputs = self.model(img_input) # assume outputs structured																																															
# Postprocess detections = []																																															
"for i, det in enumerate(outputs):"																																															
"# Each det: { 'bbox': [x1,y1,x2,y2], 'offset': [dx,dy], 'score':"																																															
conf }																																															
orig_h]																																															
"x1,y1,x2,y2 = det['bbox']"																																															
"bbox_norm = [x1/orig_w, y1/orig_h, (x2-x1)/orig_w, (y2-y1)/"																																															
# Compute global coords																																															
"if cam_gps and det.get('offset'): lat_cam, lon_cam = cam_gps dx, dy = det['offset']"																																															
"# simple approximation: 1 deg lat ~ 111km, 1 deg lon ~"																																															
111km*cos(lat)																																															
"# Convert dx,dy (in meters presumably) to lat,lon delta: dlat = (dy / 1000.0) / 111.0 # if offset in meters north"																																															
dlon = (dx / 1000.0) / (111.0 *																																															
math.cos(math.radians(lat_cam)))																																															
lat_obj = lat_cam + dlat lon_obj = lon_cam + dlon																																															
else:																																															
"# if no camera GPS or no offset, fallback to camera location"																																															
or None																																															
if cam_gps:																																															
"lat_obj, lon_obj = cam_gps"																																															
else:																																															
"lat_obj, lon_obj = None, None detections.append({"																																															
"""detection_id"": f""{uuid.uuid4()}"", ""bbox"": bbox_norm,"																																															
"""coordinates"": {""lat"": lat_obj, ""lon"": lon_obj} if lat_obj else {""lat"": 0.0, ""lon"": 0.0},"																																															
"""address"": None,  # to be filled by geocoder"																																															
"""confidence"": float(det.get('score', 0.0))"																																															
})																																															
return detections																																															
# Example usage:																																															
"# predictor = Predictor(model_path=""/app/model_weights/best_model.pth"", device='cuda')"																																															
"# result = predictor.predict_image(open('test.jpg','rb').read(),"																																															
"cam_gps=(55.7,37.6))"																																															
"(Этот псевдокод демонстрирует процесс. Реальная модель  MyGeoModel здесь абстрактна,"																																															
"outputs format assumptive. The offset to lat/lon conversion is simplistic; actual might require camera orientation. But we include as concept. If model already outputs lat/lon, then no need offset calc.)"																																															
Шаблон миграции (Alembic) – создание таблиц																																															
"-- alembic revision script (example) CREATE TABLE users (
id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
username VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
password_hash VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
role VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',
created_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE DEFAULT now(), last_login TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE
);
CREATE TABLE projects (
id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
owner_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, created_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE DEFAULT now()
);
CREATE TABLE images (
id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
source_ref VARCHAR(100), original_name VARCHAR(255),
file_key VARCHAR(255), -- key in object storage width INTEGER,
height INTEGER,
taken_date TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE,
uploaded_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE DEFAULT now(),"																																															
status VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'done'																																															
);																																															
"CREATE TABLE addresses ( id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,"																																															
"full_text TEXT NOT NULL, lat DOUBLE PRECISION,"																																															
lon DOUBLE PRECISION																																															
);																																															
"CREATE TABLE detections ( id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,"																																															
"image_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES images(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,"																																															
"address_id INTEGER REFERENCES addresses(id), lat DOUBLE PRECISION,"																																															
"lon DOUBLE PRECISION,"																																															
"confidence REAL,"																																															
"bbox_x REAL, bbox_y REAL, bbox_w REAL, bbox_h REAL, detected_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE DEFAULT now()"																																															
);																																															
"CREATE TABLE query_history ( id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,"																																															
"user_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,"																																															
"project_id INTEGER REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, query_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,"																																															
"params JSONB,"																																															
"created_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE DEFAULT now(),"																																															
"completed_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE, status VARCHAR(10),"																																															
result_count INTEGER																																															
);																																															
"CREATE INDEX idx_detection_coords ON detections(lat, lon); CREATE INDEX idx_image_project ON images(project_id); CREATE INDEX idx_query_user ON query_history(user_id);"																																															
"(Примерно так. Может ajust after design, e.g., remove lat,lon in detection if address table used, or vice"																																															
versa. Provided as a base.)																																															
Шаблон презентации (оглавление)																																															
"(Содержание слайдов, которые подготовим F1): 1. Титульный слайд: Название проекта ""GeoRag"																																															
"LCT2025"", команда, дата, логотипы. 2. Проблема и цель: Описание задачи – поиск геолокации зданий по фото, масштаб проблемы (много фото, ручной труд). 3. Решение (overview): Что делает наш сервис – pipeline от фото к координатам и адресам, UI примеры. 4. Архитектура: Диаграмма компонентов (как в разделе 4.1) с пояснениями микросервисов. 5. Алгоритм распознавания: Схема ML модели (детекция + геолокация), откуда данные (кратко про ARTS dataset?), точность, примеры результатов (например, картинка с bbox и predicted location vs actual). 6. Демонстрация сценария: скриншоты UI: загрузка фото -> результат с адресом; поиск адреса -> найденные фото. (Может анимация/GIF). 7. Масштабирование и производительность: График или цифры: X images/minute, architecture scaling plan (celery, GPU). Mention 1000/3h achieved. 8. Интеграция и экспорт: Показать OpenAPI snippet, примеры Excel экспорта (screenshot of an excel output with some data). 9. Безопасность и надежность: Bullet points: auth, roles, data encryption, offline fallback, etc. 10. Статус и планы: Что реализовано (100%) vs что можно добавить (например,"																																															
"улучшенная кластеризация зданий, offline geocoder, etc). 11. Вывод/Спасибо: Emphasize how it"																																															
"meets hackathon goals, ready for pilot, thanks etc."																																															
"(Презентация  будет  оформлена  в  привлекательном  виде,  не  перегружена  текстом,  основные"																																															
моменты визуально).																																															
Чек-листы готовности к демо/эксплуатации																																															
• Перед демо:																																															
• [ ] Все контейнеры запущены и функционируют.																																															
• [ ] Созданы учетные записи (admin:admin or given) для демонстрации.																																															
"• [ ] Заранее загружены несколько тестовых изображений (для ускорения, можно кешировать geocoder)."																																															
"• [ ] VPN/интернет работает (если нужно для geocoder), либо переключить в офлайн режим."																																															
"• [ ] Презентация открыта на ноутбуке, ссылки на репо и Swagger UI под рукой."																																															
"• [ ] План демонстрации проговорен: (например, ""сейчас я загружу фото этого здания -> вот адрес"", ""теперь найдем все фото этого адреса"" и т.д.)."																																															
"• [ ] Включен лог монитор (может, separate window with"																																															
docker-compose logs -f backend in case needed).																																															
"• [ ] Backup plan: PDF с результатами если live fails, etc."																																															
• Перед эксплуатацией (pilot deployment):																																															
• [ ] Получены коммерческие API ключи (или развернут локальный geocoder) для																																															
постоянного использования.																																															
• [ ] Настроены резервное копирование БД и хранилища.																																															
"• [ ] Определены политики хранения (например, clear images older than X)."																																															
"• [ ] Проведено обучение пользователей (документация пользователя, хоть краткая)."																																															
• [ ] Средства мониторинга (Grafana dashboards for metrics) установлены.																																															
"• [ ] Контроль доступа: созданы аккаунты каждому, администратор назначен."																																															
"• [ ] Проведен тест безопасности (Pentest) – хотя basic, но убедиться нет открытых портов, etc."																																															
"• [ ] Уточнены юридические моменты (какие данные можно сохранять, на какой срок)."																																															
Это завершает спецификацию решения.																																															
План действий на хакатон (Action Plan)																																															
"T-0 (старт, час 0): Собрание команды, уточнение требований. Разбор приоритетов: must-have (A2,"																																															
"A3, B1-B3, B5-B6), nice-to-have (история, админ, некоторые экспорты). Разделение ролей: - Team Lead/Backend Architect: (вы) – отвечает за архитектуру, БД, backend endpoints, интеграцию ML. - ML Engineer: настраивает модель геолокации, подготавливает weights, упрощает при необходимости."																																															
"- Frontend Developer: создает React app, компоненты UI. - DevOps/Integrator: настраивает Docker, CI, помогает связать frontend-backend, занимается окружением (DB, Redis, etc), пишет миграции."																																															
"T+4h: - Backend: каркас FastAPI, модели User/Project/Image/Detection, подключение к Postgres (в"																																															
"dev SQLite можно сначала), базовые endpoints (auth, projects) созданы. JWT auth работает (тестово)."																																															
"- ML: модель   Predictor class готов с заглушкой (например, возвр. фиксированные coords для"																																															
"test). Проверка, что можно загрузить пример изображения и пройти через pipeline. - Frontend: созданы страницы Login, Upload (с basic file input), Search (address input). Проекты – можно пока"																																															
"хардкод один. - DevOps: Docker-compose initial set up (Postgres up, maybe use PGAdmin to check DB,"																																															
"ensure connectivity from app). - Checkpoint: Try a simple flow: register user (maybe via DB directly, as / auth/signup not explicit), login from UI -> get 200. Hardcode skip model for now: set /predict to return dummy coordinates. Upload a dummy image -> see dummy coords in response. If this roundtrip works, basis is set."																																															
"T+8h: - Backend: implement   /uploads handling files (use FastAPI UploadFile, save to local folder"																																															
"initially). No model call yet, but simulate success. Implement /predict directly for single file (if needed). - ML: If custom model is heavy to integrate, maybe decide to use simplified approach: e.g., use EXIF GPS as output plus a random offset to simulate detection. (We likely aim to integrate actual model if possible by later). - Frontend: show the result from backend on UI (display returned coords in a list or as text). - DevOps: ensure file saving/writing has correct permissions (Docker volumes if any). - Checkpoint: End of day1 half: we should demonstrate minimal end-to-end: user login -> upload image -> get coordinate displayed. Even if coordinate is dummy or just echo camera GPS."																																															
T+12h  (конец  1го  дня):  -  Backend:  integrate  actual  ML  model  now.  ML  eng  provides  a  function																																															
"predictor.predict_image() that returns lat/lon for an image. Connect that in endpoint (maybe call asynchronously if heavy). Add geocoding: call Yandex for that lat/lon, fill address. - If geocoder issues, consider stub addresses for now (like always return ""Москва..."" for known coords) for demo, but plan to finalize next morning. - Frontend: refine UI layout, add loading spinners after upload, show results in table format with headings. Possibly add ability to add multiple images in one go (multiple selection). - DevOps: attempt to run whole system in Docker to catch config issues early. Possibly skip if slows iteration, but ensure requirements in code environment vs container (Torch installation etc). - Checkpoint: Possibly do a quick run with actual model on a test image if available, see if output looks plausible (log it). - Sleep break advisable to maintain clarity next day."																																															
T+18h (утро  2го  дня):  -  Team  sync:  what  features  remain?  Key  ones  likely  search  and  exports.  -																																															
"Backend: implement /search: tie into DB queries. If no PostGIS, implement Haversine manually for now or use simple bounding box filter + refine in Python. Implement /coordinates for bulk import: parse file (use pandas or csv for speed). - ML: finalize model integration, test on multiple images if possible to ensure stable. - Frontend: implement ResultsPage for search results differently if needed (maybe reuse same component with slight difference). - Also, implement History page if time (or stub). - DevOps: write Alembic migration for DB structure now that it's stable. Run it, test DB schema correctness. - Checkpoint: midday day2: Ideally all main functionalities coded. Quick manual tests: - Upload multiple images -> see multiple results listed. - Search address -> gets images that were uploaded if matching. - Export button triggers something (maybe stub returns file content). - No catastrophic errors in logs."																																															
T+24h (halfway): - Focus on testing & fixing: - Backend: write unit tests for critical logic (especially																																															
"geocode caching, coordinate distance, etc.) to catch any logic errors. - Frontend: open in different browsers if possible, ensure drag-drop works, JWT persists on page reload (maybe store in localStorage). - Team decides on any descoping if falling behind: e.g., if history too complex, drop it or keep minimal (just last query not a list). - Possibly get a friend or mentor to do a quick usage of UI to see if intuitive. - Checkpoint: incorporate feedback, fix UI labels (e.g., in Russian for clarity), ensure error messages propagate (like if upload fails due to file type, user gets an alert)."																																															
T+30h: - Prepare presentation content: gather screenshots of UI (maybe blur any sensitive detail if																																															
"needed). - Compute some metrics if possible: e.g., run 50 images through (maybe script) and measure time average to be confident about performance claim. - If any known flaw remains (like model accuracy low), prepare an explanation slide or a plan to improve post-hackathon. - Checkpoint: A near- final demo run: simulate pitch story with the built system. Identify any embarrassing issues and fix them now."																																															
"T+36h:  -  Buffer  time  for  finishing  touches:  -  Write  documentation  (README.md  with  how  to  run,"																																															
"ensure .env properly commented). - Clean code (remove debug prints, ensure no credentials in logs). - Frontend: maybe add a small logo or styling improvements (using a CSS framework if not earlier). - Checkpoint: Freeze feature development; focus on stability."																																															
"T+42h: - Testing & rehearsal: - Run full scenario as if at pitch: - Show login, upload, show results, search,"																																															
show export file. - Possibly pre-run geocoding queries that you'll show at demo to warm cache and avoid delays. - Team rehearses who says what if multiple presenters.																																															
T+46h: - Finalize presentation visuals and flow. Ensure all team members can articulate parts if asked																																															
"(especially Q&A likely around ""how does the model work?"" and ""how do you scale to 176k?""). - Prepare backup demonstrations (video or screenshots) in case live fails."																																															
"T+48h (submission): - Submit code to repo, ensure it runs from instructions. - Upload presentation to"																																															
submission portal. - Provide any additional docs (like this spec if needed). - Then get ready to present to judges.																																															
"Roles breakdown summary: - Backend Dev/Lead: tasks at T+4,8,12,18, etc mainly backend items, DB,"																																															
"integration. - Frontend Dev: tasks at T+4 (setup pages), T+12 (UI polish), T+18 (search UI), T+36 (CSS/UX finishing). - ML Engineer: tasks at T+4 (choose model approach), T+12 (get model running with sample), T+18 (optimize & finalize), plus earlier if training needed (maybe not in hack, likely using existing model). - DevOps: tasks at T+0 (architecture plan input), T+8 (initial docker config), T+18 (db migration, environment issues fix), T+30 (metrics gather), T+42 (ensuring final deploy smooth). - All: testing at multiple phases, collaborative debugging."																																															
"By following this plan, our team can systematically implement and verify all requirements within the"																																															
"hackathon timeframe, while also preparing a compelling presentation for the final pitch."
